# BUGS — reported by operators / foreman / helpdesk, fixed by the top model (code owner)
Format: `- [ ] time author: symptom | evidence (event line / probe output, coords) | where you think it is`. ONE line per bug; grep before adding (no duplicates).
Done = tick `[x]` + the fix in a few words. A new open line wakes the top model (`ops/escalate.sh`); lines whose author contains "top model" do not. World 1: `attic/world1/BUGS.md`.

- [ ] 09-21 06:5xZ top model (nether): **`params.bare` banks the barter CARGO.** `bare_handed {job:"nether_barter", banked:{diamond_shovel:1,diamond_axe:1,diamond_pickaxe:1,diamond_sword:1,**gold_ingot:20**}}` at 06:58:24Z - army.js `bareDown` correctly strips valuables for an experimental trip, but gold is what a barter bot goes there to SPEND, so a bare barter bot can cross with 0 ingots and trade nothing, silently. Worked around in jobs_nether.js (the crossing now re-draws gold after bareDown and refuses to go under 16, and checks the worn gold piece); the real fix is for `bareDown` to take a keep-list from the job (`params.cargo`) so a job can name what is not loot.
- [ ] 09-21 06:2xZ end engineer: **a `needsNight` night is still skipped by the OWN-BED cycle, so every night expedition loses its night.** 06:26:05Z dusk, `end_hunt_1` (params.needsNight) active and the sleeper correctly stayed up (`Ayame job_slice sleeper r:"guard"` 06:26:57Z) — but four bots lay in their own beds at 06:26:45-48 (`slept {own:true, ms:1509/1519/1800/1501}`: Yukina+Himari on end_hunt_1, Akari cavity_base, Noa road_village) and the world jumped from t~13400 to t=706 inside 40 s. The `c.real ? 1500 : 20000` hold in `bedtime()` is meant to prevent exactly this and does not on Paper 26.2 — four overlapping 1.5 s sleeps were enough. Net effect: that night lasted ~55 s and the enderman squad reported `ender_dry`. **It is INTERMITTENT, not constant**: the very next night (06:47:08-06:57:20Z, t 12069 -> 23300) ran its FULL ~11 500 ticks with the same `needsNight` job active and **nobody lay down at all** - no `slept`/`in_bed`/`bed_skip` - because those four bots had just had their turn and `BED_DUE_MS` is 6 h. So the night is lost only at a dusk where some bot's own-bed turn falls due, i.e. ~1/3 of the army on the first night after a worker restart and once per 6 h per bot after that. Look for the cause in the bed turn, not in `needsNight` (which works) | evidence: `grep -E '"slept"|"in_bed"' bots/army/results.jsonl` 06:26Z, BOARD.md headers 06:26:05 (time 11827 night) -> 06:27:24 (time 706 day) | army_jobs.js `bedDue`/`bedtime`: on a real night do not lie down AT ALL (the bed turn is for the respawn point, which a click already sets — `activateBlock` without `bot.sleep`), and put a job with `params.needsNight` into the `/^(delegate|sleeper|steps|scout)$/` exclusion so a night squad is never walked 150 blocks home at dusk.
- [ ] 09-21 05:5xZ top model (nether): **we build blind over the void.** `nether_barter_road` lost TWO bots into the lava sea from the same cell of the lane's first leg (Erika 05:46:19Z -51,27,-75, 31 iron + 5 diamond; Chika 05:52:29Z -52,27,-75, 15 iron + 2 diamond; tasks `nether barterspot: 4/3 placed, 0 cleared`, `steps` 18 and **0**). Every pass reports `unloaded:1053 of 1053` — `bot.blockAt` returns null for the whole work area 30 s after arrival — and `jobs_nether.js cellOK()` treats an unreadable cell as DONE while `edgeNear`/`noFloor` treat it as a drop. So a bot stands at the rim of a 70-block void placing blocks it cannot read back, and the second bot fell having taken no `safeStep` at all. Job PAUSED, bots 0. Fix needs (a) `moves.bridgeTo` for the void span instead of hand-placed cells from a stand at the rim, (b) no cell worked while its chunk reads null, (c) the connected-air measurement the owner asked for, to tell a 3-cell step from an 80-block chasm before a lane is planned.
- [ ] 09-20 16:2xZ recover engineer: a SECOND death that lands while core/recover.js is already walking to the first one loses its own decision - no `recovered`/`recover_failed`/`recover_skipped` is ever written for it, so that kit is silently dropped. Tsumugi died 15:56:38 at -346,40,-534 (ravine floor), was rescue-killed 15:57:15 at -346,69,-533 six blocks from her bed while the first trip was still running (`recover_failed {at:[-346,40,-534],why:"no route: stopped 29 m short"}` at 15:57:28 = the abort on `bot.__armyDied`), `kitted respawn` 15:57:48 - and nothing about the second kit (a diamond_sword, 6 blocks away) in her ledger afterwards | evidence: `grep '"bot":"Tsumugi"' bots/army/results.jsonl` 15:56-15:59; army_worker.js overwrites `bot.__armyDeathInfo` per death and core/index.js drops an alert older than 30 s (A.kitUp runs in between) | core/recover.js onDeath/onTick, or the worker consuming only the LAST death info

- [x] (opus 13:50Z: DISPATCHER CHURN — 1203 ASSIGN/h = 24 job changes per bot per hour, a walk + a handover bank + an interrupted slice each, median stint 115 s, 50 % under 2 min; top flows muster<->tidy_spawn 285+284/h, fill_ravine_n 62 in/63 out while it held 0-1 of 16. Causes measured in dispatcher.js: (1) tenure was undone by the second staffing pass and by `last[n] !== S.fallback`, so a tick in the sponge laundered any bot free; (2) a declining job was refilled with the next bot for ever; (3) the sponge was an unbounded dump for ~30 bots it can employ 8-10 of; (4) a `produces` head-count could collapse in ONE tick — west_terrace_cut_2 dug dirt 644 -> 6403 past a target of 2048 and threw 18 bots to muster, which stopped the digging and called them back (85 out / 71 in per hour). FIX in bots/army/dispatcher.js: `holdFast`+`busy` (tenure holds in BOTH passes, broken only by prio >= 96, the demand floor, or an idle heartbeat task), `drainable` (no squad under half its head-count), source-ordered pool (muster/fallback/overflow first, then the lowest-priority squad with the most bots, nearest), `refuses` (3 declines of the same job park that pairing 20 min), REST rule with backoff (3 same-reason declines + no output in 5 min -> the JOB rests 10->30 min), `pickOverflow`/`roomFor`/`overFit` (surplus bots join a terrain squad with open cells instead of the sponge), `headFall` (a head-count falls at most 1 bot/min), and `assignsPerHour`/`churnFlows` in status.json + a CHURN line every 10 min. RESULT 1203 -> 237 assigns/h (4.7 per bot-hour), 0 bots at muster, best window 9 moves/10 min. Also in the same file: dimension filter (`DIMOF`/`jobDim`, `settings.nether.returnJob`, DIM HOLD log), herd demand from `audit_pen.outside > 3`, untargeted producers get their floor not half a squad, `maxFronts = max(10, online/4)` with a FRONT CUT line.)
